# 只增 ID 总账（`docs/dev/ids.md`）

> **从此仓库里这一份是唯一的账；设计稿那份（`D:/Code/FluxEcho-campus/redesign/LEDGER.md`）冻结。** 0.17.0 开版时（refactor §③ F01，2026-10-27）由设计稿原样入库：下面从「全城唯一的 ID 账」起都是设计稿的原文，只在本段加了入库说明。以后加行、定版（「新增」→「本版」→「已发布」）都只改这一份（0.17.0 的接口提交已把首发正好是 0.17 的 76 行改成「本版」；首发写成几个版本的三行——§1.1 的码段 29–31、42–47 和 §11 的 `feB02`…`feB17`——仍是「新增」，等各自的卡定了再分行），由整合者在接口提交和标签提交里改（§22.3）；设计稿里的同名表不再更新。
>
> **机器可读**：§19 的 JSON 由 `java tools/IdsCsv.java` 导出成同目录的 `ids.csv`（一行一个 ID：类、节、值、含义、归属、首发版本、状态、出处）；改了本文的 JSON 就重跑它，`IdsCsvTest` 守着两者一致。逐版的计划数据（首发的新形状楼、环段数、可见星数、main 估值）在 `src/test/resources/plan/batches.csv`，`BatchPlanTest` 读它。

> 全城唯一的 ID 账。0.17.0 开版第一件事（refactor §③ F01）原样入库为 `docs/dev/ids.md`，同一份数据导出 `src/test/resources/plan/ids.csv`、`batches.csv`；入库之后以仓库那一份为准，本稿只读。
>
> **基线**：仓库 `D:/Code/FluxEcho` = 标签 0.16.0（`1cc50f2`，工作树干净）。凡标「已发布」的行都对过源码（只读：`logic/Parts`、`logic/ModuleCores`、`nexus/NexusNet`、`Config`、`campus/Campus`、`campus/BuildJob`、`nexus/TileMultiblock`、`city/TileCityModule`、`library/BookPacket`、`logic/*Shape`、`quests/build_quests.py`、两份 lang）；没有改仓库，也没有跑 gradle。
>
> **依据**（同级的先列）：`refactor/FINAL.md` §④ 4.1–4.13（底稿；4.12 的裁定照用）；`ui/FINAL.md` §2.9（消息号唯一账）；`style/FINAL.md` §3.8.3（部件码总账，码值照用，版本按 refactor §4.8）；`narrative/FINAL.md` §3–§9 与 `research-tree.csv`；`masterplan/FINAL.md` §3、§6、§10；21 份楼稿与 9 份组 README 的 §6 / §7；`DECISIONS.md`。refactor 没裁的，按「已发布的不动 → 先登记的优先 → 同一件东西全城一个码 → 相邻码段留余量」由本账裁定，编号 **L01–L22**（§20.1），每条都进了差异表。核验第 1 轮（`critique/ledger-check-1.md`）的改账编号 **V1-01–V1-24**（§20.3），差异表续到第 192 行；r-verify-1 复核结果见文末「修订记录」，差异表续到第 198 行。核验第 2 轮（`critique/ledger-check-2.md`）的改账编号 **V2-01–V2-10**（§20.4），差异表续到第 224 行，复核记录见文末 r-verify-2。P0 复查第 1 轮（`critique/r3-rules.md`）的改账编号 **R3-01**（§20.5），差异表续到第 233 行，修订记录见文末 r-p0-1。P0 复查第 2 轮（`critique/r4-gameplay.md` #2、`critique/r4-rules.md` #2 #6、`critique/r4-feasibility.md` #3）的改账编号 **R4-01–R4-11**（§20.6），本账裁定续 L22，差异表续到第 246 行，修订记录见文末 r-p0-2。

## 怎么读

| 列 | 含义 |
|---|---|
| 值 | 码、号、键、注册名或前缀；一格里多个值用空格隔开，都是同一行的同一状态 |
| 首发 | 第一次随哪个版本发布（`≤0.16` = 已在 0.16.0 标签里；`—` = 未排版本）。题材件、配置、任务等「随楼」的，取楼的首发版本（refactor §2.1） |
| 出处 | `H` = 仓库 0.16.0 源码；`R` = refactor/FINAL.md；`U` = ui/FINAL.md；`S` = style/FINAL.md；`N` = narrative/FINAL.md；`RT` = narrative/research-tree.csv；`M` = masterplan/FINAL.md；`D` = DECISIONS.md；`B/<组>/<楼>` = buildings/<组>/<楼>.md；**`Lnn`** = 本账裁定 |
| 状态 | **已发布**：在 0.16.0 标签里，一个字都不改。**新增**：设计已定、排了首发版本；打那一版标签时由整合者改成「已发布」。**本版**（入库后加的状态，§22.2 第 4 条的「新增 · 本版」）：首发就是正在做的版本；那一版的接口提交把它的「新增」行改成「本版」，打标签的提交再改成「已发布」。**预留**：号留着、没排版本，用前先在本账写明用途（对应 refactor §4.1 的「预留」）。**作废**：设计稿用过、但永不发布的值；**号和名永不复用**（已发布后退役的值记「已发布」并在含义里写「退役」，如 GT offset 3、12） |

## 1. 部件码

### 1.1 码段总表（0–1023）

部件码是 `PartBlocks` 的双向映射：码 ↔ (方块, meta)。≤ 127 的码走 `BUILD_FX`（byte），≥ 128 只走 `BUILD_FX2`（short，S→C 12）。码值照 style §3.8.3，首发版本照 refactor §4.8。

## 2. 模块核心与 `Buildings` 行（行号 = `SkinTable` 的 key 索引，只能往后加）

`city/kit/Buildings` 在 0.19 一次写好 22 行（refactor §4.2）；「新形状首发」= 该行换掉 `LegacyKit` 的版本。码列 = 部件码（§1.2）。行号 = 叙事建筑编号 = 记分项 `feBxx` 的号（refactor 写了 00–04、20、21，05–19 按 **L21** 补全）。状态列指这一行的模块键 / 核心 / TE 是否已在 0.16.0 发布；`Buildings` 类本身 0.19 新建，行号从那时起冻结。

`BuildingKit.since()` 的值照本表「新形状首发」列写成 `"0.xx"`（不带补丁号：写 `"0.26"`，不写 `"0.26.0"`；`""` = 还没有新形状，即 `LegacyKit`，refactor §4.2）；楼稿里已有的 `since()` 除机库一处外都是这个写法（V2-05）。

## 4. 网络消息

### 4.1 `fluxecho_nx`（`NexusNet`）消息种类

号只由本表（= ui §2.9.1）分配；没到版本的号只有常量、没有处理器，`ClientSinks` 收到不认识的号就丢弃。

| 方向 | 号 | 名 | 载荷 / 含义 | 归属 | 首发 | 出处 | 状态 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C→S | 0 | `ASK_MAP` | 要园区地图 | 引擎 | ≤0.16 | H `NexusNet` | 已发布 |
| C→S | 1 | `START` | 开始研究（载荷 中枢位置 `Dim` `X` `Y` `Z` + 研究 `Id`；`NexusNet.handle` → `startResearch`；不是「开工」，V1-01） | 引擎 | ≤0.16 | H `NexusNet` | 已发布 |
| C→S | 2 | `CANCEL` | 取消研究（载荷只有中枢位置；`NexusNet.handle` → `cancelResearch`；不是营造的取消，那个走 `VIEW_ACT` 的 `build.cancel`，§17.2；V1-01） | 引擎 | ≤0.16 | H `NexusNet` | 已发布 |

## 5. NBT 键

### 5.1 基类保留键（`DescKeysTest` 的黑名单）

规则：每座楼的新键要么收进子复合键，要么用楼专属前缀；楼稿已定的照楼稿；全部写进 `BuildingKit.nbtKeys()`，且不得与本表重名。基类以后加键要扫全部楼的 `nbtKeys()`。

| 值 | 含义 | 归属 | 首发 | 出处 | 状态 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `F` `On` `T` `O` `Sh` `Facing` `Formed` `FormedTime` `Shape` `OwnerLeast` `OwnerMost` `OwnerName` | `TileMultiblock` 基类键（`Sh` = 形状名） | 引擎 | ≤0.16 | H `nexus/TileMultiblock` | 已发布 |
| `P` `Dk` `Rv` `Ep` `Samples` `Unit` `Renovated` `Dim` `X` `Y` `Z` | `TileCityModule` 基类键 | 引擎 | ≤0.16 | H `city/TileCityModule` | 已发布 |
| `B` `Bi` `E`（书本栈内 `At`） | `BookPacket` 写进 `TileCityModule` / 书库描述包的样本书 | 引擎 | ≤0.16 | H `library/BookPacket`；**L13** | 已发布 |
| `Docked` `Nexus` `Slot` | `TileModule` 基类键 | 引擎 | ≤0.16 | H | 已发布 |
| `Ov`（描述包） | 覆层种类（4 座灯座 / 4 个锚点各 1 字节）：全城模块的描述包保留给覆层 | 中枢组 / 统筹 | 0.21 | R §4.5；B/nexus/nexus §7；**L09** | 新增 |

| `Rx` | 玩家二次确认可拆的外来机器：list `[{P: long 打包坐标, B: string 方块注册名}]`。外来机器 = `Probe.te` 为真（有 TE、不是矿、不是补给口 / 回收箱）且不是本 mod 城市部件；**矿石（`Probe.gtOre`）永不进**。`TerrainRule` 重铸分支只对坐标和注册名都命中 `Rx` 的外来机器判 CLEAR，其余外来机器 BLOCKED；同一坐标换了方块即失效；每项任务完成时删掉落在它范围里的条目；由动作 `build.allow_foreign`（§17.2，C14 两次确认）写入。**只在中枢 tile 的 `Campus` 子表**：不进核心物品（`Campus.Carried`）、不进 `Campus.writeSync`（P03 要显示的确认状态随勘测摘要下发，M §10.2.1 一「同步」行）。已对 §5.2 `Campus` 现有键（含 `R` `Rc` `Rj` `Raw` `Recycle` `Ro`）、核心物品 `Campus` 复合的键、`writeSync` 键、`BuildJob` 任务键（含 `Fl2`）、`Form` 键（`Pf` `Pd` `Sd` `Rs` `Ab` `Fsn` `Gts`）查过不撞，仓库 0.16.0 源码没有 `"Rx"` 字面量 | 总平面 | 0.19 | M §10.2.1 三与「版本」段、§10.3 `Campus` 行；R4-04 | 新增 |
| 任务 `Form.Gts` | 削顶段 `grade:top:<k>`（§13.1）的边界：int[5] = `aFrom` `aTo` `rFrom` `rTo` `yTo`（园区 A / R 坐标，含两端；整行成段时 `rFrom` / `rTo` 取掩膜在这几行的 R 全幅；`yTo` = 这一段勘测到的最高实心格的 Y）。勘测完成、提供这一段时确定性地算出并写入，**之后不再变**；`planFor` 只读它，不按现场地形重算——削到一半重新加载，计划和进度位集都不错位（facts/campus §1.8）；缺 = 不是削顶任务。`GroundSplit` 只靠计划键后缀 `#k`、不存 NBT，削顶段不能照搬：它的边界取决于削之前的地形。放在 `BuildJob.Form`（客户端照同一组字段算出和服务端一样的计划，投影要它；**L22**），所以同 `Ab` / `Fsn` 一样平铺进任务 NBT **和** `Campus.writeSync` 的同步复合；物理键 `Gts` 已对 §5.2 `BuildJob` 任务键（0.16 的 52 个 + `Fl2`）、`writeSync` 键（0.16 的 33 个）、`Form` 键（`Pf` `Pd` `Sd` `Rs` `Ab` `Fsn`）、`Campus` 子表键（含 `Gl` `Gn` `Gq` `Gk`）查过不撞，仓库 0.16.0 源码没有 `"Gts"` 字面量；`TaskKeysTest` 守（§22.4） | 总平面 | 0.22 | M §10.2.1 二「计划」行、§10.3.1；facts/campus §1.8；H `BuildJob.Form`；**L22**；R4-05 | 新增 |

同义字段合并见 **L06**；划掉的键永不使用。

## 7. 配置节与键

一行一个节（或一组键）；默认值写在括号里。每节一个 `XConfig` 类，`Config.java` 只有登记表（红区）。旧键一个不动。

| 值 | 含义 | 归属 | 首发 | 出处 | 状态 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|

    "entities": {"section": "3.4", "title": "实体与 GT 机器 ID", "cols": ["value", "meaning", "owner", "since", "source", "status"], "rows": [
      {"value": "echo_dragon（模组实体 id 0，类 EntityEchoDragon）", "meaning": "猎场守门者亲战的回响龙。签字前不注册：0.24 不写 EntityEchoDragon、不调 registerModEntity，FluxEcho 0.24 起仍一个模组实体都不注册（同 rain_veil 的条件注册；IdFreezeTest 断言预留值没被注册）；用户签猎场未决 11（BRIEF §5「不做敌人」的例外）后由 0.X.1 注册，注册名、id、类名照本行，那时改「新增」（§21 未解决 26）", "owner": "血猎组", "since": "0.X.1（条件：用户签猎场未决 11）", "source": "R §4.10；L14；B/blood-hunt/hunting_ground §6.5、§8 #26、未决 11；R4-02", "status": "预留"},
      {"value": "GT 机器 ID 24530–24569（general.firstMachineId + offset；offset 3、12 已退役）", "meaning": "FluxEcho 的 GT 机器；本轮一个都不加", "owner": "引擎", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H core/MachineId；R §4.10", "status": "已发布"},
      {"value": "GT 机器 ID 24520–24529", "meaning": "FluxDepths 的 GT 机器（任务书引用）", "owner": "FluxDepths", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H quests/build_quests.py", "status": "已发布"}
    ]},
    "facades": {"section": "3.5", "title": "跨楼门面（类名；签名按 refactor §4.3 冻结）", "cols": ["value", "meaning", "owner", "since", "source", "status"], "rows": [

      {"value": "PowerBook", "meaning": "upkeepOf(kit)、net(team)；generator(PowerSource) 0.21 加；冻结 0.20", "owner": "底座（包 GTWirelessBackend）、各发电楼", "since": "0.20", "source": "R §4.3 #4", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "ComputeBudget", "meaning": "source(ComputeSource)、rate(team, Sink)；冻结 0.20", "owner": "底座、智子、观星台", "since": "0.20", "source": "R §4.3 #5", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "ShipTide（含 TideBank）", "meaning": "level(ship, worldTime)（本船潮位，含稳潮）、mode(ship)、windowTide(ship, worldTime)（海的潮位：唤潮之后，稳潮 / 锁潮 / 放大潮之前；起航窗口、乡忆 ③、井壁起航线与潮尺只读它，任何情况下不拿 level 代替——开着稳潮满月午夜 level 只到 1.10）、nextAbove(threshold, from)（下一次潮位过阈值的世界时）、factor(level, mode, m)（楼的潮系数 τ楼）；lock / amp 0.22 加；call(ship) 0.26 加；冻结 0.20", "owner": "底座、阙、气象站", "since": "0.20", "source": "R §4.3 #6；N §9.4；B/endgame/ascension §3.5；B/power/README §3.1、§3.9；R4-01", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "ChecklistRows", "meaning": "row(n) → ChecklistRow{progress, ready, explainKey}；冻结 0.20", "owner": "每座楼一行", "since": "0.20", "source": "R §4.3 #7", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "VaultIntake", "meaning": "offer(team, dim, ItemStack, Source, simulate) → Result、offerFluid(...)；空值 NO_VAULT；冻结 0.22", "owner": "二向箔仓", "since": "0.20（草签）", "source": "R §4.3 #8", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "ResonanceBoost", "meaning": "speed(team, key)、parallel、surcharge、tideMode、inPhase；冻结 0.22", "owner": "谐振塔（阙）", "since": "0.20（草签）", "source": "R §4.3 #9", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "PetalSink", "meaning": "offer(team, petals)、price(team)；空值 拒收；冻结 0.22", "owner": "魔力泉阵", "since": "0.20（草签）", "source": "R §4.3 #10", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "ShieldLedger", "meaning": "sp(team)、spend(team, kind, cost)、full(team)；overcharge 0.23 加；列阵 0.26 加；冻结 0.21", "owner": "回响穹顶", "since": "0.21", "source": "R §4.3 #11", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "AspectStores", "meaning": "take(team, aspect, n)；冻结 0.23", "owner": "秘法庭", "since": "0.22（草签）", "source": "R §4.3 #12", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "HostileCensus / WardVolume", "meaning": "census(dim)、inside(team, x, y, z)；冻结 0.24", "owner": "回响穹顶", "since": "0.21（草签）", "source": "R §4.3 #13", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "Greenwood", "meaning": "active(team)；冻结 0.24", "owner": "血契坛", "since": "0.20（草签）", "source": "R §4.3 #14", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "SailState", "meaning": "rigged(team)、nightBonus(team)；冻结 0.25", "owner": "天光帆阵", "since": "0.20（草签）", "source": "R §4.3 #15", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "RainCover", "meaning": "covered(team, box)；冻结 0.25", "owner": "回响穹顶", "since": "0.21（草签）", "source": "R §4.3 #16", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "IgnitionStands", "meaning": "stand(ship, k)；unlock(ship, k, player) 0.28 加；冻结 0.25", "owner": "跃迁环", "since": "0.20（草签）", "source": "R §4.3 #17", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "CovenantLink、HuntLink、ArchiveLink", "meaning": "血猎组要的三个跨楼读数（offerMeat(team, n)、pearlsPerHour(team)、isShelved(team, mobKey)）；名和方法待 0.24 接口提交前 RFC（未解决 16）", "owner": "血契坛 / 猎场 / 档案馆", "since": "—", "source": "B/blood-hunt/README 未决 6；B/blood-hunt/hunting_ground 未决 16", "status": "预留"},
      {"value": "ShieldBook、ComputeBook、TideBook、FoilVault、VaultInput", "meaning": "提案二与旧楼稿的门面名 → ShieldLedger / ComputeBudget / ShipTide；取料走 VaultIntake 的追加方法（未解决 16）", "owner": "—", "since": "不发", "source": "R §4.12；B/nexus/nexus 未决 26", "status": "作废"}
    ]},
    "net_messages": {"section": "4.1", "title": "fluxecho_nx（NexusNet）消息种类", "cols": ["dir", "num", "name", "payload", "owner", "since", "source", "status"], "rows": [
      {"dir": "C→S", "num": "0", "name": "ASK_MAP", "payload": "要园区地图", "owner": "引擎", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H NexusNet", "status": "已发布"},
      {"dir": "C→S", "num": "1", "name": "START", "payload": "开始研究（载荷 中枢位置 Dim X Y Z + 研究 Id；NexusNet.handle → startResearch；不是「开工」，V1-01）", "owner": "引擎", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H NexusNet", "status": "已发布"},
      {"dir": "C→S", "num": "2", "name": "CANCEL", "payload": "取消研究（载荷只有中枢位置；NexusNet.handle → cancelResearch；不是营造的取消，那个走 VIEW_ACT 的 build.cancel，§17.2；V1-01）", "owner": "引擎", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H NexusNet", "status": "已发布"},
      {"dir": "C→S", "num": "3", "name": "LIFT", "payload": "光梯", "owner": "引擎", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H NexusNet", "status": "已发布"},
      {"dir": "C→S", "num": "4", "name": "ASK_VIEW", "payload": "{target(x,y,z,dim), view, page, rev}", "owner": "界面组", "since": "0.18", "source": "U §2.9.1；R §4.4", "status": "新增"},
      {"dir": "C→S", "num": "5", "name": "VIEW_ACT", "payload": "{target, view, action, args(NBT)}：全部按钮、ctl.set、各楼动作键", "owner": "界面组", "since": "0.18", "source": "U §2.9.1；R §4.4", "status": "新增"},
      {"dir": "C→S", "num": "6", "name": "NEXUS_LOOK", "payload": "{GLANCE | BEARING}：仰头右键的退路，原型不过才注册", "owner": "中枢组", "since": "—（默认不注册）", "source": "U §2.9.1；R §4.4", "status": "预留"},
      {"dir": "S→C", "num": "0", "name": "MAP", "payload": "园区地图；只增字段 Rev、Obs、Xiang、Coords、RouteN（已解算航段数 0–7）（0.18）；航段字段不叫 Seg：Seg 是 fluxecho_voyage 的「已补环段」位集（§5.6），同名异义（V1-02）", "owner": "引擎 / 界面", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H；U §2.9.1；V1-02", "status": "已发布"},
      {"dir": "S→C", "num": "1", "name": "RESULT", "payload": "结果", "owner": "引擎", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H", "status": "已发布"},
      {"dir": "S→C", "num": "2", "name": "BUILD_FX", "payload": "施工特效（码 ≤ 127，byte）", "owner": "引擎", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H", "status": "已发布"},
      {"dir": "S→C", "num": "3", "name": "BUILD_STATE", "payload": "施工状态", "owner": "引擎", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H", "status": "已发布"},
      {"dir": "S→C", "num": "4", "name": "LIB_DELTA", "payload": "书库增量", "owner": "引擎", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H", "status": "已发布"},
      {"dir": "S→C", "num": "5", "name": "UNIT_DELTA", "payload": "单元增量", "owner": "引擎", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H", "status": "已发布"},
      {"dir": "S→C", "num": "6", "name": "LIFT_RIDE", "payload": "光梯乘坐", "owner": "引擎", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H", "status": "已发布"},
      {"dir": "S→C", "num": "7", "name": "SKIN_TABLE", "payload": "512 格内全部园区的盒子表", "owner": "风格组", "since": "0.17", "source": "U §2.9.1；R §4.4", "status": "本版"},
      {"dir": "S→C", "num": "8", "name": "SKIN_DELTA", "payload": "一条盒子表条目", "owner": "风格组", "since": "0.17", "source": "U §2.9.1；R §4.4", "status": "本版"},
      {"dir": "S→C", "num": "9", "name": "VIEW", "payload": "{view, page, rev, ic, nbt}", "owner": "界面组", "since": "0.18", "source": "U §2.9.1；R §4.4", "status": "新增"},
      {"dir": "S→C", "num": "10", "name": "TOAST", "payload": "{kind, langKey, args, severity, sigilKey, pos?}", "owner": "界面组", "since": "0.18", "source": "U §2.9.1；R §4.4", "status": "新增"},
      {"dir": "S→C", "num": "11", "name": "HIGHLIGHT", "payload": "{pos, color, seconds}", "owner": "界面组", "since": "0.18", "source": "U §2.9.1；R §4.4", "status": "新增"},
      {"dir": "S→C", "num": "12", "name": "BUILD_FX2", "payload": "同 BUILD_FX，码用 short[]（码 ≥ 128 才走）", "owner": "风格组", "since": "0.20", "source": "U §2.9.1；R §4.4", "status": "新增"},
      {"dir": "S→C", "num": "13", "name": "DOME_HIT", "payload": "{种类, 守护号, 方位, 仰角, 强度, 位置}", "owner": "守护组", "since": "0.21", "source": "U §2.9.1；R §4.4", "status": "新增"},
      {"dir": "S→C", "num": "14", "name": "ROOT_NET", "payload": "本队本维度被扎根的采集器坐标表（原「暂记 11」作废）", "owner": "发电组", "since": "0.25", "source": "U §2.9.1；R §4.4", "status": "新增"},
      {"dir": "S→C", "num": "15", "name": "DROPLET_LEG", "payload": "{机库坐标, 序号, 种类, 起点, 终点, 起始 tick, 时长, 镜面档}", "owner": "守护组", "since": "0.26", "source": "U §2.9.1；R §4.4", "status": "新增"},
      {"dir": "S→C", "num": "16", "name": "LEAP", "payload": "{T0, ShipId, tier, stage, index, part[]}（原 {stage, index, t} 作废）", "owner": "终局组", "since": "0.28", "source": "U §2.9.1；R §4.4", "status": "新增"},
      {"dir": "S→C", "num": "17 起", "name": "—", "payload": "下一个新号；只在本账登记后才可用", "owner": "—", "since": "—", "source": "本账", "status": "预留"},
      {"dir": "（不是消息）", "num": "—", "name": "VOYAGE 段", "payload": "中枢 SkinTable 条目 extra 里的一段（池子、点火台、阶段、T0、ShipId、档位、钟）；字节 15–18 是 ShipId（string dim:x:y:z，§5.6）的 32 位散列，完整值走 LEAP（散列算法见 §21 未解决 24）；环的段态是环条目 extra 24 字节", "owner": "终局组", "since": "0.19 起", "source": "U §2.9.1；B/endgame/leap_ring §3.16、修订 F；V2-08", "status": "新增"},
      {"dir": "（不加）", "num": "—", "name": "RING_STATE、ORBIT、WHISPER、HOLO_PICK、HOLO_MAPS、SCOPE_AIM、SITE_SURVEY", "payload": "各提案曾要的种类，已并进描述包 / SkinTable / 视图，永不注册", "owner": "—", "since": "—", "source": "B/endgame/README §3；B/compute/README §4；B/nexus/drift_capsule", "status": "作废"}
    ]},
    "net_other": {"section": "4.2", "title": "其它通道与终端页号（不加种类）", "cols": ["channel", "num", "meaning", "owner", "since", "source", "status"], "rows": [
      {"channel": "fluxecho（EchoNet）", "num": "0–2", "meaning": "回响机器的现有种类", "owner": "引擎", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H；R §4.4", "status": "已发布"},
      {"channel": "fluxecho_gate", "num": "现有", "meaning": "光门", "owner": "引擎", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H", "status": "已发布"},
      {"channel": "FluxLite fluxlite C→S", "num": "0–4", "meaning": "CONNECTOR_REQUEST、CONNECTOR_EDIT、CC_REQUEST、CC_ACTION、TERM_REQUEST（FluxLite 仓库）", "owner": "FluxLite", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H（FluxLite）", "status": "已发布"},
      {"channel": "FluxLite fluxlite S→C", "num": "0–3", "meaning": "CONNECTOR_DATA、CC_DATA、HOLO_DATA、TERM_DATA", "owner": "FluxLite", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H（FluxLite）", "status": "已发布"},
      {"channel": "FluxLite 终端页号", "num": "0–4", "meaning": "NETWORK、MACHINES、CODEX、RESEARCH、NEXUS", "owner": "FluxLite", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H（FluxLite TerminalView）", "status": "已发布"},
      {"channel": "FluxLite 终端页号", "num": "5", "meaning": "潮汐", "owner": "界面组", "since": "0.18", "source": "U §6（U3b）；R §4.4", "status": "新增"},
      {"channel": "FluxLite 终端页号", "num": "6", "meaning": "典籍", "owner": "界面组", "since": "0.18", "source": "U §6（U3b）；R §4.4", "status": "新增"},
      {"channel": "FluxLite 终端页号", "num": "7", "meaning": "归途", "owner": "界面组", "since": "0.18", "source": "U §6（U3b）；R §4.4", "status": "新增"}
    ]},
    "nbt_base": {"section": "5.1", "title": "基类保留键（DescKeysTest 的黑名单）", "cols": ["value", "meaning", "owner", "since", "source", "status"], "rows": [
      {"value": "F On T O Sh Facing Formed FormedTime Shape OwnerLeast OwnerMost OwnerName", "meaning": "TileMultiblock 基类键（Sh = 形状名）", "owner": "引擎", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H nexus/TileMultiblock", "status": "已发布"},
      {"value": "P Dk Rv Ep Samples Unit Renovated Dim X Y Z", "meaning": "TileCityModule 基类键", "owner": "引擎", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H city/TileCityModule", "status": "已发布"},
      {"value": "B Bi E（书本栈内 At）", "meaning": "BookPacket 写进 TileCityModule / 书库描述包的样本书", "owner": "引擎", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H library/BookPacket；L13", "status": "已发布"},
      {"value": "Docked Nexus Slot", "meaning": "TileModule 基类键", "owner": "引擎", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H", "status": "已发布"},
      {"value": "Ov（描述包）", "meaning": "覆层种类（4 座灯座 / 4 个锚点各 1 字节）：全城模块的描述包保留给覆层", "owner": "中枢组 / 统筹", "since": "0.21", "source": "R §4.5；B/nexus/nexus §7；L09", "status": "新增"}
    ]},
    "nbt_campus": {"section": "5.2", "title": "园区（中枢 tile 的 Campus 子表）、核心物品、任务、账本", "cols": ["value", "meaning", "owner", "since", "source", "status"], "rows": [
      {"value": "Ac Bl BuildIn Campus Declined Dim F Fb Fe Ft Gk Gl Gn Gq Hint Job K Ko Kr Ledger Md Mi N Nb Nc Nq Nr Ns Oc Ol Op P Pa Parts Ph Pk Pl Pos Pq Ps Pv Queue R Raw Rc Recycle Rj Ro S Sg Sites Sk Skip Sn Spoils St Te To V W", "meaning": "中枢 tile Campus 子表与 Campus.writeSync 的现有键（Sk = 跳过受阻位）", "owner": "引擎", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H campus/Campus", "status": "已发布"},
      {"value": "Skn", "meaning": "每座楼最后已知的盒子表状态和供电位（不能叫 Sk）", "owner": "风格组", "since": "0.17", "source": "R §4.5；S §3.8.3(f)", "status": "本版"},
      {"value": "Mp", "meaning": "总图版本：0 旧图（缺省）、1 一叶归舟", "owner": "总平面", "since": "0.19", "source": "R §4.5", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "Rk", "meaning": "有重铸记录的模块键列表（记录本体在 fluxecho_recast）", "owner": "总平面", "since": "0.19（字段）/ 0.22（写入）", "source": "R §4.5", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "Ledger.LegAcct", "meaning": "腿账：穹顶记腿、机库扣腿", "owner": "守护组", "since": "0.21", "source": "B/guard/README §4", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "Dispatch、HoloAwake", "meaning": "沙盘调度表；沙盘醒着的位", "owner": "算力组（沙盘）", "since": "0.23", "source": "B/compute/holo_table §7", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "核心物品 Campus 复合：Mp、Rk", "meaning": "拆核心带走，放回原处接着用", "owner": "总平面", "since": "0.19", "source": "R §4.5", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "Bc Bl ByL ByM C Cc Cl Cy D Fd Fe Fl Fr Fs Ft Go Gw I K Kc Ko Kr L Mc Na Nb Nc Nq Nr Op P Pa Pd Pf Pk Ps Ro Rs S Sc Sd Sg Sk St Te Tf To Ts Tu U Un V", "meaning": "BuildJob 任务 NBT 的现有键（V = 3、GRADE_V = 2，本轮不提）", "owner": "引擎", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H campus/BuildJob", "status": "已发布"},
      {"value": "任务 Fl2", "meaning": "飞行中格子的码（short），只在出现 ≥ 128 时写", "owner": "风格组", "since": "0.20", "source": "R §4.5", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "任务 Form.Sn（物理键 Fsn）", "meaning": "原地换形的目标形状名（reform:<号>），string；缺 = 老计划。BuildJob.Form 平铺写进任务 NBT（BuildJob.write）和 Campus.writeSync 的同步复合（客户端 Campus.View.read 用 Form.read 读同一个复合）；不能叫 Sn——Sn 是 writeSync 已发布的计划阶段数（byte，View.read 用 getByte(\"Sn\") 读）。Fsn 已对 §5.2 的 BuildJob 任务键（含新增 Fl2）和 Campus.writeSync 的现有键查过不撞，TaskKeysTest（R3-01 的 FormKeysTest 扩名，R4-09）守（§22.4）；各稿写「Form.Sn」的都指这个字段，NBT 里的键一律是 Fsn", "owner": "重构组", "since": "0.21", "source": "R §4.5；H BuildJob.Form.write、Campus.writeSync / View.read；R3-01", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "任务 Form.Ab", "meaning": "重燃计划：byte，1 = 舱重燃来的 establish（舱格 keep-out + 最后一步 ABSORB）；缺 / 0 = 老计划。BuildJob.Form 平铺写进任务 NBT，物理键 Ab，不撞 §5.2 的 BuildJob 现有键；也不撞 Campus.writeSync 的现有键（Form.write 同样平铺进同步复合，TaskKeysTest 守，R3-01、R4-09）；不是 Pf（Pf = 0.16 已发布的吊舱浮空基准高度 F）", "owner": "中枢组", "since": "0.19", "source": "B/nexus/drift_capsule §3.7.4、未决 14；B/nexus/nexus §3.3；H BuildJob.Form；V1-03；R3-01", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "账本 Parts2", "meaning": "成品部件（short 码），只在出现 ≥ 128 时写；读档两个键都读", "owner": "风格组", "since": "0.20", "source": "R §4.5", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "Rx", "meaning": "玩家二次确认可拆的外来机器：list [{P: long 打包坐标, B: string 方块注册名}]。外来机器 = Probe.te 为真（有 TE、不是矿、不是补给口 / 回收箱）且不是本 mod 城市部件；矿石（Probe.gtOre）永不进。TerrainRule 重铸分支只对坐标和注册名都命中 Rx 的外来机器判 CLEAR，其余外来机器 BLOCKED；同一坐标换了方块即失效；每项任务完成时删掉落在它范围里的条目；由动作 build.allow_foreign（§17.2，C14 两次确认）写入。只在中枢 tile 的 Campus 子表：不进核心物品（Campus.Carried）、不进 Campus.writeSync（P03 要显示的确认状态随勘测摘要下发，M §10.2.1 一「同步」行）。已对 §5.2 Campus 现有键（含 R Rc Rj Raw Recycle Ro）、核心物品 Campus 复合的键、writeSync 键、BuildJob 任务键（含 Fl2）、Form 键（Pf Pd Sd Rs Ab Fsn Gts）查过不撞，仓库 0.16.0 源码没有 \"Rx\" 字面量", "owner": "总平面", "since": "0.19", "source": "M §10.2.1 三与「版本」段、§10.3 Campus 行；R4-04", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "任务 Form.Gts", "meaning": "削顶段 grade:top:<k>（§13.1）的边界：int[5] = aFrom aTo rFrom rTo yTo（园区 A / R 坐标，含两端；整行成段时 rFrom / rTo 取掩膜在这几行的 R 全幅；yTo = 这一段勘测到的最高实心格的 Y）。勘测完成、提供这一段时确定性地算出并写入，之后不再变；planFor 只读它，不按现场地形重算——削到一半重新加载，计划和进度位集都不错位（facts/campus §1.8）；缺 = 不是削顶任务。GroundSplit 只靠计划键后缀 #k、不存 NBT，削顶段不能照搬：它的边界取决于削之前的地形。放在 BuildJob.Form（客户端照同一组字段算出和服务端一样的计划，投影要它；L22），所以同 Ab / Fsn 一样平铺进任务 NBT 和 Campus.writeSync 的同步复合；物理键 Gts 已对 §5.2 BuildJob 任务键（0.16 的 52 个 + Fl2）、writeSync 键（0.16 的 33 个）、Form 键（Pf Pd Sd Rs Ab Fsn）、Campus 子表键（含 Gl Gn Gq Gk）查过不撞，仓库 0.16.0 源码没有 \"Gts\" 字面量；TaskKeysTest 守（§22.4）", "owner": "总平面", "since": "0.22", "source": "M §10.2.1 二「计划」行、§10.3.1；facts/campus §1.8；H BuildJob.Form；L22；R4-05", "status": "新增"}
    ]},
    "nbt_core": {"section": "5.3", "title": "中枢、搁浅舱、档案馆（tile 与描述包）", "cols": ["value", "meaning", "owner", "since", "source", "status"], "rows": [

    "nbt_module": {"section": "5.4", "title": "各楼模块 tile 与描述包", "cols": ["value", "meaning", "owner", "since", "source", "status"], "rows": [
      {"value": "Eco*、Ark*（前缀）", "meaning": "生态穹全部新键", "owner": "生态组", "since": "0.20", "source": "R §4.5；B/eco/eco_dome §7", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "Valves Burn Pool Sluice Fuel Lever Store Furnace Cap Charge Bell", "meaning": "根位：潮汐井的阀档 Valves、阀芯余能 Burn、潮池 Pool、潮闸 Sluice、燃料缓冲 Fuel、钟 Bell；Lever（「烧 / 存」拨杆）、Furnace（炉）、Cap Charge（电容环）、Store（存料格 / 源料口）到 0.27（地脉核、触底 source_reach）才用，随井一并登记，0.21 起空着（V2-10）", "owner": "发电组", "since": "0.21", "source": "B/power/tidal_well §7、A ⑤；V2-10", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "NetSeen", "meaning": "根位：根网已见", "owner": "发电组", "since": "0.25", "source": "B/power/tidal_well §7", "status": "新增"},

      {"value": "Sp Sov Wd Ws Gr Sl Bk Tn Lg Ct Hk Wx Gs", "meaning": "回响穹顶：SP（×100）、超充（原稿 Ov，改 Sov）、七项守护档位 / 待位、罩径、减载线、破盾到期、张力、日志、今日计数、最近拦截方位…", "owner": "守护组", "since": "0.21", "source": "B/guard/dome_generator §7；L09", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "穹顶描述包 Sp Wd Ws Bk Gr Tn Hk", "meaning": "同步给吊舱与 HUD", "owner": "守护组", "since": "0.21", "source": "B/guard/dome_generator §7", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "Wall{Lv, Lf[{It/Fl, N}], Cp, Fg} Bq Bk Md Hl Hn Tb Ks Rf Ws", "meaning": "二向箔仓：43 幅箔墙、来源缓冲、箔匣、印 / 密度 / 去向、化录、二维化名单、升维台页、保量表、破界压进、…", "owner": "门与仓", "since": "0.22", "source": "B/gate-vault/dual_foil_vault §7", "status": "新增"},

      {"value": "智子描述包 Su Sm Sf Sc Sr Sx Sd", "meaning": "同步", "owner": "算力组", "since": "0.23", "source": "B/compute/sophon_array §7", "status": "新增"},

      {"value": "Cd Cn Wo Dl Tc Sc", "meaning": "气象站：三种冷却、催晨冷却、最后一道天令、天气盘、唤潮槽 EU、预约", "owner": "守护组", "since": "0.26", "source": "B/guard/weather_station §7", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "气象站描述包 Wfc", "meaning": "日程摘要（8–12 天 × 4 段，≈ 200 字节，只发给 64 格内的人；不撞已发布的 Wf）", "owner": "守护组", "since": "0.26", "source": "B/guard/weather_station §7.3、未决 15；V1-14", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "Dp Sa Sc Pn Md Lv Hd Xp Fl Kn Gf", "meaning": "水滴机库：Dp 8 颗水滴各 {状态, 当前航段, 货舱}、Sa 各扇颗数（byte[8]）、Sc 32 格 × 2 位的格状态（long）、Pn 钉住掩码（byte[8]）、Md 巡态 0–3（驻巢 / 夜巡 / 常巡 / 列阵）、Lv 闸（0 收回 / 1 放出）、Hd 暂存 ≤ 27 组、Xp 经验点（int）、Fl 滤网（模式 byte + ≤ 9 物品）、Kn 今夜清理（int）、Gf 已赠送（byte）（V2-06）", "owner": "守护组", "since": "0.26", "source": "B/guard/droplet_hangar §7.3、修订 27；V2-06", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "Sat[4]{T, St, At} Srv{P, D, F} Rly[16]{Dim, X, Y, Z, Port} RlyBuf[2] Chain Ap ApM Dust{Night} Rt Edge Core2 WhT", "meaning": "轨道锚 / 冠：卫星、测绘、中继、锚链、孔径、承尘、去向、边匣、源核座、窗语计时", "owner": "终局组", "since": "0.27", "source": "B/endgame/orbital_anchor §7；B/endgame/README §3", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "RlyEu[4]", "meaning": "中继口电口的缓冲 → 电口已删（锚稿玩法评审 #4），从未发布", "owner": "—", "since": "不发", "source": "B/endgame/orbital_anchor 修订记录 #22；V1-15", "status": "作废"},
      {"value": "BeatAt BeatN Partial SkipMask StableSince Lit Altar Cap Charge Target AutoFeed", "meaning": "源点之心：心跳、瓣、承尘坛、池子、目标、自动喂", "owner": "发电组", "since": "0.27", "source": "B/power/source_heart §7", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "（无新键）", "meaning": "跃迁环控制器只存核心和覆层（基类键）；状态在 fluxecho_voyage", "owner": "终局组", "since": "0.28", "source": "B/endgame/README §3", "status": "新增"}
    ]},
    "nbt_item": {"section": "5.5", "title": "物品 NBT 与实体数据", "cols": ["value", "meaning", "owner", "since", "source", "status"], "rows": [
      {"value": "foil：Lv K Ls Sm Ix", "meaning": "二向箔：一种物品 + long 数量、封样、索引", "owner": "门与仓", "since": "0.22", "source": "B/gate-vault/dual_foil_vault §7", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "rift_foil：Night", "meaning": "破界箔压成的那一夜", "owner": "门与仓", "since": "0.22", "source": "B/gate-vault/dual_foil_vault §7", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "droplet：Mirror Kills Born", "meaning": "水滴：镜面、击杀、出厂日", "owner": "守护组", "since": "0.26", "source": "B/guard/droplet_hangar §7", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "resonance_fork：Gate{x, y, z, dim} Team", "meaning": "定音叉：绑定的阙、团队", "owner": "门与仓", "since": "0.22", "source": "B/gate-vault/resonance_gate §3.4.3、§7.3；V1-14", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "root_card：Dim X Y Z Kind Team Veins", "meaning": "根须卡：采集器坐标、种类（采集器 / 流体泵）、团队、矿脉", "owner": "发电组", "since": "0.25", "source": "B/power/tidal_well §3.4；V1-14", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "source_echo：Night Moon Wh", "meaning": "源点残响：Night 落下那一夜的世界日、Moon 那一夜的月相（冠写，心按 Night 去重、按 Moon 分瓣）；Wh byte = 1：窗语残响（举晶落下的 4 颗，第 17 夜起的回声夜也写；孔径按小时出的不写），箔仓的破界压只收带 Wh 的（V2-01）。物品上的 Wh 是旗标，和 fluxecho_voyage.Wh（已落窗语句数，§5.6）不是一张表", "owner": "终局组", "since": "0.27", "source": "B/endgame/orbital_anchor §3.8；B/power/source_heart §3.3；B/gate-vault/dual_foil_vault §3.4、§3.10、E5；V1-14、V2-01", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "实体数据 feBout", "meaning": "猎场亲猎真身的 \"<场地号>:<场次号>\"（随实体存盘；MobEvents 见它就跳过）", "owner": "血猎组", "since": "0.24", "source": "B/blood-hunt/hunting_ground §3、§7", "status": "新增"}
    ]},
    "nbt_voyage": {"section": "5.6", "title": "fluxecho_voyage 字段（每座中枢一条「船」，键 = 中枢维度 + 坐标；读档缺字段用默认）", "cols": ["value", "meaning", "owner", "since", "source", "status"], "rows": [
      {"value": "ShipId", "meaning": "string dim:x:y:z；舱阶段先用舱坐标，重燃时改写成中枢坐标", "owner": "叙事", "since": "0.18", "source": "N §3.6", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "Team", "meaning": "UUID：中枢主人团队", "owner": "叙事", "since": "0.18", "source": "N §3.6", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "Day0", "meaning": "long：放下搁浅舱的世界总时间", "owner": "叙事", "since": "0.18", "source": "N §3.6", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "Xiang", "meaning": "list {N, Day}：七段乡忆和恢复的那一天。老存档按已达状态补授 ①②（团队并集里 recall_echo 已完成 → ①、anchor 已完成 → ②；Day = 补授那天，顺序 ①②；XiangBackfill 在中枢 stood() 和第一次打开中枢时幂等调用，每段只有一条）；键与结构不变（R4-11）", "owner": "叙事", "since": "0.18", "source": "N §1.6.2、§3.6、§9.4；B/nexus/nexus §3.3", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "Glance", "meaning": "24 位掩码：看过的件（舱 1–12、中枢 13–24；位号永不改）", "owner": "中枢组", "since": "0.18", "source": "B/nexus/drift_capsule §3", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "Built", "meaning": "list {Key, Day}：每座建筑（形态）第一次成型的日子，按先后（S03 / S18、沙盘预演、露台画廊读这个顺序）；Key = 总平面位置表的 key：Buildings 键（§2），同一行多形态的用 键:形态（root:tidal_well root:deep_root root:ley_core orbital_anchor:source_crown；单写 orbital_anchor = 轨道锚），写进存档后不改（V2-07）", "owner": "叙事", "since": "0.18", "source": "N §3.6；M 位置表；V2-07", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "Shares[20]", "meaning": "byte[]：跃升清单 20 行的进度 0–100", "owner": "叙事", "since": "0.19", "source": "N §3.6", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "Seg", "meaning": "位集：已补的段", "owner": "终局组", "since": "0.19", "source": "B/endgame/leap_ring §7", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "SegSub", "meaning": "位集：按替代来源补的段", "owner": "终局组", "since": "0.19", "source": "B/endgame/leap_ring §3、§7", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "Bell", "meaning": "上次自鸣的世界日", "owner": "终局组", "since": "0.20", "source": "B/endgame/leap_ring §7", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "TideMode / TideSteady", "meaning": "byte / bool：全船默认的读潮方式（常态 / 锁潮 / 放大潮；按楼的锁 / 放大潮在阙 NBT Al，经 ResonanceBoost.tideMode 读）、稳潮开关（ctl.set 目标 voyage.steady）", "owner": "叙事 / 发电组", "since": "0.21", "source": "N §3.6、§5.9", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "Route[7]", "meaning": "long[]：每段航线已解算的 FC（智子 RouteSolver 写）", "owner": "叙事 / 算力组", "since": "0.23", "source": "N §3.6；B/compute/sophon_array §7", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "Rehearsed / Rehearsals / RehRow", "meaning": "bool / int / 行：预演成功、成功次数、给环读的行", "owner": "算力组（沙盘）", "since": "0.23", "source": "N §3.6；B/compute/holo_table §7", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "Signed", "meaning": "list {U, Name, T}：签了契的人（T = 世界时；搁浅天数由 T 与 Day0 算）", "owner": "叙事 / 血猎组 / 终局组", "since": "0.24", "source": "N §3.6；B/endgame/ascension；L06", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "Coords / CoordDay[7]", "meaning": "int 位掩码 / long[]：七颗航标与定星日", "owner": "叙事 / 算力组", "since": "0.25", "source": "N §3.6；B/compute/observatory §7", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "MoonDay", "meaning": "满月夜记录", "owner": "算力组", "since": "0.25", "source": "B/compute/observatory §7", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "TideCall", "meaning": "long：唤潮生效到哪一个世界日", "owner": "叙事 / 守护组", "since": "0.26", "source": "N §3.6；B/guard/weather_station §7", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "Escort", "meaning": "int：团队水滴累计击杀（第 17 行护航）", "owner": "守护组", "since": "0.26", "source": "B/guard/droplet_hangar §7", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "Wh / WhD", "meaning": "byte 0–16 / long：已落窗语句数、最后一句的日子（WhisperRite 写；观星台只读）", "owner": "终局组", "since": "0.27", "source": "B/endgame/README §3；L06", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "Xiang5", "meaning": "乡忆 ⑤「海岸」的锚侧旗标", "owner": "终局组", "since": "0.27", "source": "B/endgame/README §3", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "Capacitor", "meaning": "long：跃升电容汇总读数（上限 2×10¹²）", "owner": "叙事", "since": "0.27", "source": "N §3.6", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "CapP / CapR / CapG", "meaning": "long[2] / byte[2] / bool：两墩池子、充能闸档、护城", "owner": "终局组", "since": "0.27", "source": "B/endgame/leap_ring §7", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "BankOn", "meaning": "两墩电容启用（ring:bank 完成）", "owner": "终局组", "since": "0.27", "source": "B/endgame/leap_ring §7", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "Pads / PadBy[4]", "meaning": "4 位：点火台已解锁（位 0 阳墩、1 阴墩、2 观星台、3 气象站）/ 谁、哪天", "owner": "终局组", "since": "0.28", "source": "B/endgame/leap_ring §7；B/guard/weather_station §3；L06", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "Leap{St, T0, Shot, Part[], Tier}", "meaning": "跃升阶段（无 / 待航 / 倒计时 / 跃升中 / 已跃升）、对齐的潮顶世界时、分镜、参与者、档位", "owner": "终局组", "since": "0.28", "source": "B/endgame/ascension §7；L06", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "Bearing", "meaning": "归航坐标已合（第 14 行 = Coords 7 位全 ∧ Bearing）", "owner": "算力组", "since": "0.28", "source": "B/compute/observatory §7", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "Cele", "meaning": "long：上一次满潮庆典的世界日", "owner": "终局组", "since": "0.28", "source": "B/endgame/ascension §7；L06", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "Leaps", "meaning": "int：跃升次数（leap 只授予一次，给「远航」留位）", "owner": "叙事", "since": "0.28", "source": "N §3.6", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "HomeRoom / HomeFit", "meaning": "原乡露台房号、生成进度", "owner": "终局组", "since": "0.28", "source": "B/endgame/ascension §7", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "Vol6:[{U, Day, By}] / Stay:[{U, Day}]", "meaning": "谁得了哪一版终卷（By = leap / gate）", "owner": "终局组", "since": "0.28", "source": "B/endgame/ascension §3.10、§7", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "Whisper", "meaning": "叙事稿的窗语句数 → 用 Wh", "owner": "—", "since": "不发", "source": "N §3.6；L06", "status": "作废"},
      {"value": "State / T0（顶层）", "meaning": "叙事稿的跃升阶段 → 用 Leap.St / Leap.T0", "owner": "—", "since": "不发", "source": "N §3.6；L06", "status": "作废"},
      {"value": "Celebrated", "meaning": "→ 用 Cele", "owner": "—", "since": "不发", "source": "N §3.6；L06", "status": "作废"},
      {"value": "Pact", "meaning": "血契坛稿的签契名单 → 用 Signed", "owner": "—", "since": "不发", "source": "B/blood-hunt/blood_covenant；L06", "status": "作废"},
      {"value": "Ign", "meaning": "观星台稿的点火台位 → 用 Pads 位 2 + PadBy[2]", "owner": "—", "since": "不发", "source": "B/compute/observatory；L06", "status": "作废"}
    ]},

    "config": {"section": "7", "title": "配置节与键", "cols": ["value", "meaning", "owner", "since", "source", "status"], "rows": [
      {"value": "general：firstMachineId enableDefaultRecipes", "meaning": "0.16 现有", "owner": "引擎", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H Config", "status": "已发布"},
      {"value": "quests：install", "meaning": "0.16 现有", "owner": "引擎", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H", "status": "已发布"},
      {"value": "bees：enabled imprintTicks imprintEuPerTick incubateTicks incubateEuPerTick assembleTicks assembleEuPerTick honeyPerPrincess dronesWithPrincess honeyPerDrones dronesPerBatch fertilizerPerSaplings saplingsPerBatch honeyPerButterfly butterfliesPerBatch", "meaning": "0.16 现有（15）", "owner": "引擎", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H", "status": "已发布"},
      {"value": "thaumcraft：enabled essentiaEuPerUnit essentiaUnitsPerShard pedestalEuPerCentivis pedestalCentivisPerTick extractionModuleCentivisPerTick pedestalBufferEU wirelessModuleRange linkModuleEuPerTick hologramRange insightTicks insightEuPerTick", "meaning": "0.16 现有（12）", "owner": "引擎", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H", "status": "已发布"},
      {"value": "bloodmagic：enabled lpPerTick euPerLp lpPerMeat altarRange altarHeight", "meaning": "0.16 现有（6）；血契坛读 lpPerMeat，不另设 covenant.lpPerMeat", "owner": "引擎", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H；L07", "status": "已发布"},
      {"value": "mobs：enabled euPerHealth minTicks allowBosses bossMultiplier dropBlacklist", "meaning": "0.16 现有（6）；猎场读 euPerHealth（楼稿写的 euPerHp 不存在）", "owner": "引擎", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H；L07", "status": "已发布"},
      {"value": "crops：enabled imprintTicks imprintEuPerTick seedTicks seedEuPerTick sticksPerSeed", "meaning": "0.16 现有（6）", "owner": "引擎", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H", "status": "已发布"},
      {"value": "botania：enabled lvManaPerTick euPerMana lvManaPerPetal poolRange poolHeight hologramRange", "meaning": "0.16 现有（7）", "owner": "引擎", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H", "status": "已发布"},
      {"value": "codex：tooltipHints", "meaning": "0.16 现有", "owner": "引擎", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H", "status": "已发布"},
      {"value": "nexus：enabled crystalGrit upkeepEuPerTick computePerSecond manifestEuPerTick recordCooldownTicks innerRingRadius libraryUpkeepEuPerTick moduleUpkeepEuPerTick effects effectRange campusEnabled archiveEnabled buildClearPerTick buildEuPerBlock buildEuPerClear buildClearHeight buildFillDepth buildMinSpacing buildRequireSky buildKeepSpoils buildClearAll buildClearable buildBlocked buildProjection buildGhostRange buildGhostCells buildEffects buildArcs", "meaning": "0.16 现有（29）；moduleUpkeepEuPerTick 留作 LegacyKit.upkeep() 默认值", "owner": "引擎", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H；R §4.6", "status": "已发布"},
      {"value": "effects：machineEffects flowTrails effectRange hologramRange", "meaning": "0.16 现有（4）", "owner": "引擎", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H", "status": "已发布"},
      {"value": "ae2：enabled", "meaning": "0.16 现有", "owner": "引擎", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H", "status": "已发布"},
      {"value": "gates：enableRecipe viewRange liveView realView holdBase dimensionId providerId", "meaning": "0.16 现有（7）", "owner": "引擎", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H", "status": "已发布"},
      {"value": "Config.VERSION", "meaning": "\"0.15.0\" → \"0.19.0\"（Config.upgrade 只改仍等于旧默认的值）", "owner": "重构组", "since": "0.19", "source": "H；R §4.6", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "render.occlusion（gate）、render.skyLayer（world）", "meaning": "原型过了在 0.X.1 改 depth / sky", "owner": "风格组", "since": "0.17", "source": "R §4.6", "status": "本版"},
      {"value": "style.hullBrightness", "meaning": "舟壳亮度", "owner": "风格组", "since": "0.17", "source": "S §3.8.3(f)；R §4.6", "status": "本版"},
      {"value": "client.uiClassic、client.reduceMotion", "meaning": "旧界面开关（只 0.18 有效，之后保留为不起作用的键）、少动画", "owner": "界面组", "since": "0.18", "source": "U §6；R §4.6", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "client.haze、client.uiSounds", "meaning": "雾白幕、界面音效（界面稿已列，重构稿漏列）", "owner": "界面组", "since": "0.18", "source": "U §2、§6；L07", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "client.hudCapsule", "meaning": "P66「状态胶囊」开关：手持已绑定终端时左上角的 HUD 胶囊（不是搁浅舱；原写「舱 HUD」是误读，V1-04）；跟 U5b 走，U5b 被砍挪 0.24 时同挪（R §2.2）", "owner": "界面组", "since": "0.23（U5b）", "source": "U §5 P66、§6.1 U5b；R §2.2；V1-04", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "starmap.key（M）、starmap.homeKey（Home）", "meaning": "航图热键", "owner": "界面组 / 叙事", "since": "0.18", "source": "N §9.5；R §4.6", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "route.*、ui.xiangSeen.*", "meaning": "航线显示、乡忆已读", "owner": "界面组", "since": "0.18", "source": "R §4.6", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "research.costProfile（gt / strict）", "meaning": "选 research.json 的花费档", "owner": "叙事", "since": "0.18", "source": "N §9.5；R §4.6", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "voyage.enabled（true）、voyage.capsuleOnFirstJoin（true）", "meaning": "跃升总开关；新玩家首次进入领舱（老存档补发共用「已发」位）", "owner": "叙事", "since": "0.18", "source": "N §9.5；R §4.6", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "capsule.sonarRadiusChunks（3）、capsule.sonarCooldownTicks（24000）", "meaning": "回声定位", "owner": "中枢组", "since": "0.18", "source": "B/nexus/drift_capsule §6；R §4.6", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "capsule.rekindleTicks（600）、capsule.patchDays、capsule.surveyColumnsPerTick（2048）", "meaning": "重燃；勘测（残图、勘测卡随 refactor B00b 在 0.19 交付，V2-02）", "owner": "中枢组", "since": "0.19", "source": "B/nexus/drift_capsule §6；R §4.6、§3.2 B00b；V2-02", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "campus.newMap（true）、campus.safeGround（true）、campus.antiFreeze（true）、campus.hills（refuse）", "meaning": "新总图开关与地面。campus.hills 是取值表（R4-06）：refuse（默认，0.19）= 新园区掩膜里有截顶以上的悬山时舱判「不宜」、[重燃] 禁用、提示换址；重铸（老园区，没有址可换）里 refuse 按 grade 读；0.19–0.21 只有 refuse（读到别的值按 refuse）；grade（0.22）见本节末行；「未知」列两边都挡（新园区挡重燃，重铸挡第 0 批）", "owner": "总平面", "since": "0.19", "source": "M §10.3、§10.2.1 四；R §4.6", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "ui.interactChips", "meaning": "交互芯片", "owner": "界面组", "since": "0.19", "source": "R §4.6", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "nexus.libraryUpkeepEuPerTick", "meaning": "默认 128 → 64（迁移）", "owner": "中枢组", "since": "0.19", "source": "B/nexus/library §6；R §4.6", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "archive.*：cardUpkeepEuPerTick activeCardTicks writeTicks writeEuPerTick pressCooldownTicks indexStep indexDiscount indexCap bindCrystals bindCategories bindTicks crossDimFcPerCard shoreAutoShelved", "meaning": "档案馆（压印 / 索引随 0.21、装订随 0.27 起作用）", "owner": "中枢组", "since": "0.19", "source": "B/nexus/library §6；L07", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "compute.*：capsule（1）nexusPerPhase（20）sophonPerRack（10 / 20 / 40 / 80）nightBonus（1.25）clockBonus（1.05）manifestFC（33）routeShare（0.5）", "meaning": "算力来源；智子产量只用 sophonPerRack", "owner": "叙事 / 中枢组", "since": "0.20", "source": "N §9.5；R §4.6、§4.12", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "generators（节）", "meaning": "发电额定、燃料值、时段 / 天气系数、潮池秒数；按楼分子节 generators.root.*、generators.sails.*、generators.heart.*", "owner": "发电组", "since": "0.20", "source": "N §9.5；B/power/README §6", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "upkeep.*：idleFraction（0.125）workFraction（0.25）tierDoubling（true）", "meaning": "统一电规则", "owner": "叙事", "since": "0.20", "source": "N §9.5", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "tides.*：enabled spring（0.2）neap（0.05）wellFactor（2）steadyFactor（0.5）echoMachines（true）", "meaning": "潮汐", "owner": "叙事", "since": "0.20", "source": "N §9.5；D", "status": "新增"},

      {"key": "city/beat", "condition": "feB04 ≥ 6；feB19 ≥ 2；feB18 ≥ 2 / 4", "line": "line/city", "since": "0.27", "source": "N §7.2", "status": "新增"},
      {"key": "city/launch", "condition": "feB04 ≥ 6；feB19 ≥ 2；feB18 ≥ 2 / 4", "line": "line/city", "since": "0.27", "source": "N §7.2", "status": "新增"},
      {"key": "city/offer", "condition": "feB04 ≥ 6；feB19 ≥ 2；feB18 ≥ 2 / 4", "line": "line/city", "since": "0.27", "source": "N §7.2", "status": "新增"},
      {"key": "city/ignite", "condition": "feB20 ≥ 5", "line": "line/city", "since": "0.28", "source": "N §7.2", "status": "新增"},

      {"key": "city/hunt_duel", "condition": "feHunt ≥ 2（守门者之证）；用户签猎场未决 11 之前 hunt.duels 默认关，这条「完成后才显示」，签字后改常显（key、条件不变；R4-02）", "line": "line/city", "since": "0.24", "source": "B/blood-hunt/hunting_ground §7、§8 #26；L10", "status": "新增"}
    ]},
    "scoreboard": {"section": "11", "title": "记分项（原版记分板；楼的阶梯只升不降）", "cols": ["value", "meaning", "owner", "since", "source", "status"], "rows": [
      {"value": "feRec", "meaning": "记录总数（并集）", "owner": "叙事", "since": "0.17", "source": "N §7.4", "status": "本版"},
      {"value": "feCat", "meaning": "有记录的类别数", "owner": "叙事", "since": "0.17", "source": "N §7.4", "status": "本版"},
      {"value": "feShore", "meaning": "「滩」记录数", "owner": "叙事", "since": "0.17", "source": "N §7.4", "status": "本版"},

      {"value": "feCoord", "meaning": "星坐标数 0–7", "owner": "叙事", "since": "0.17", "source": "N §7.4", "status": "本版"},
      {"value": "fePhase", "meaning": "中枢相位 0–5", "owner": "叙事", "since": "0.17", "source": "N §7.4", "status": "本版"},
      {"value": "feRes", "meaning": "研究完成总数", "owner": "叙事", "since": "0.17", "source": "N §7.4", "status": "本版"},
      {"value": "feRoute", "meaning": "航线主干进度 0–13（anchor 1 … crossing 13）", "owner": "叙事", "since": "0.17", "source": "N §7.4", "status": "本版"},
      {"value": "feLeap", "meaning": "跃升阶段 0–6", "owner": "叙事 / 终局组", "since": "0.17", "source": "N §7.4", "status": "本版"},
      {"value": "feList", "meaning": "清单完成行数 0–20（实时，可降）", "owner": "叙事", "since": "0.17", "source": "N §7.4", "status": "本版"},
      {"value": "feB00", "meaning": "舱：1 放下、2 回声定位用过、3 重燃", "owner": "叙事", "since": "0.17", "source": "N §7.4", "status": "本版"},
      {"value": "feB01", "meaning": "中枢：1 成型、2 第一次显化、3 份额备齐", "owner": "叙事", "since": "0.17", "source": "N §7.4", "status": "本版"},
      {"value": "feB02、feB03、feB05–feB09、feB11–feB17", "meaning": "1 成型、2 第一次真正使用（城志）、3 份额备齐", "owner": "叙事 / 各楼", "since": "0.17（随楼有值）", "source": "N §7.4", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "feB04", "meaning": "根位 7 级：井成型、大潮满阀 1 分钟、深根成型、根网绑定第一台、地脉核成型、第一颗源点物质、份额备齐", "owner": "叙事 / 发电组", "since": "0.17", "source": "N §7.4", "status": "本版"},
      {"value": "feB10", "meaning": "智子 4 级：成型、插满一架、展开度 2、份额备齐", "owner": "叙事 / 算力组", "since": "0.17", "source": "N §7.4", "status": "本版"},
      {"value": "feB18", "meaning": "桅顶 5 级：锚成型、第一颗卫星、冠成型、第一句窗语、份额备齐", "owner": "叙事 / 终局组", "since": "0.17", "source": "N §7.4", "status": "本版"},
      {"value": "feB19", "meaning": "心 5 级：成型、第一次运转、份额备齐、heart_2、heart_3", "owner": "叙事 / 发电组", "since": "0.17", "source": "N §7.4", "status": "本版"},
      {"value": "feB20", "meaning": "跃迁环 6 级：断环立起、第一次补段、≥ 12 段、拱心合拢、第一次解锁点火台、份额备齐", "owner": "叙事 / 终局组", "since": "0.17", "source": "N §7.4", "status": "本版"},
      {"value": "feHunt", "meaning": "猎场亲猎：1 第一次试猎赢、2 守门者之证（feB13 不插级）", "owner": "血猎组", "since": "0.24", "source": "B/blood-hunt/hunting_ground §7；L10", "status": "新增"}
    ]},

    "plan_keys": {"section": "13.1", "title": "计划键", "cols": ["value", "meaning", "owner", "since", "source", "status"], "rows": [
      {"value": "establish、forum、library、establish:<p>、grow:<p>、establish:outer、establish:third、refresh、repair:establish、repair:<场地>、module:<键>@<场地>、release、deepen", "meaning": "0.16 的计划键（establish:third 永不提供）", "owner": "引擎", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H BuildPlan / BuildJob", "status": "已发布"},
      {"value": "retire:<slot>", "meaning": "只指 0.9.2 书库大厅退役；含义永远不变", "owner": "引擎", "since": "≤0.16", "source": "H；R §4.12", "status": "已发布"},
      {"value": "repair:ground:<名>（如 repair:ground:keel）", "meaning": "地面任务被拆后的修复（沿 0.16 BuildJob.REPAIR + 计划键的写法；归门 / 舰心门站位件缺格时排它）", "owner": "总平面 / 中枢组", "since": "0.21", "source": "B/nexus/nexus §3.14.3、未决 26 ②；H BuildJob.REPAIR；V1-06", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "establish:ring", "meaning": "断环壳（只引用 R1 码）", "owner": "终局组", "since": "0.19", "source": "R §4.11", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "ground:keel、ground:aft、ground:berth:1、ground:plot@72", "meaning": "龙骨地面、后舷（aft）、船位 1、档案馆地块（显示名照 M §6.1：aft「后舷」、stern「船尾」、berth「船位」；「泊位」是 0.16 SKY 31/33/35/37 的旧称，不给 berth 用）", "owner": "总平面", "since": "0.19", "source": "R §4.11；M §6.1；V1-08", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "ring:<段号>", "meaning": "补段（段 = 环段号；ring:12 单列）", "owner": "终局组", "since": "0.19", "source": "R §4.11", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "ground:plot@73", "meaning": "生态穹地块", "owner": "总平面", "since": "0.20", "source": "R §4.11", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "ring:fit", "meaning": "墩内件（establish:ring 完成后自动排队）", "owner": "终局组", "since": "0.20", "source": "R §4.11；B/endgame/leap_ring §3", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "ground:bow、ground:bay:n / ground:bay:s、ground:berth:2、ground:aft:water", "meaning": "船首、两湾、船位 2、后舷放水", "owner": "总平面", "since": "0.21", "source": "R §4.11", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "reform:<场地号>", "meaning": "原地换形：reform:9（井 0.21 / 根 0.25）、reform:8（带腿穹顶 0.21）、reform:0（阙 0.22）；将来的 PLOT 原地换形同理（如 reform:73）。目标形状名记在任务的 Form 字段，物理键 Fsn（§5.2，不是 Sn；R3-01）", "owner": "总平面 / 各楼", "since": "0.21", "source": "R §4.11；M §10.3.1", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "recast:<旧场地号>", "meaning": "换场地重铸：HALL 2 / 4 / 6、PAV 1 / 3 / 5 / 7、OUTER_DIAG 11 / 13 / 15 / 17、SKY 30 / 32 / 34 / 36", "owner": "总平面", "since": "0.22", "source": "R §4.11、§4.12；M §10.3.1", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "ground:unlay", "meaning": "拆塔柱和新图不要的铺地（代替 recast:outer）", "owner": "总平面", "since": "0.22", "source": "M §10.3.1", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "ground:plot@76、ground:plot@77", "meaning": "箔仓、泉阵地块", "owner": "总平面", "since": "0.22", "source": "R §4.11", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "ground:berth:3、ground:plot@78、@80、@81", "meaning": "船位 3、秘法庭、智子、沙盘地块", "owner": "总平面", "since": "0.23", "source": "R §4.11", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "ground:plot@82、@83", "meaning": "血契坛、猎场地块", "owner": "总平面", "since": "0.24", "source": "R §4.11", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "ground:sky", "meaning": "天台（天光池、机库池、雨池、南盆落点；对角栈桥归各楼模块任务）", "owner": "总平面", "since": "0.25", "source": "R §4.11；M §11.1", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "ground:plot@84、@85、@86、@87", "meaning": "天台四座的「庭院」地面任务：总平面没有内容（天台的地面全在 ground:sky，M 未决 24），号留着、不提供（V1-05 由「新增」改「预留」）", "owner": "总平面", "since": "—", "source": "R §4.11；M §6.1、未决 24；V1-05", "status": "预留"},
      {"value": "ground:stern、ground:ley、ground:berth:4、ground:plot@89", "meaning": "船尾（回望丘）、地脉线、船位 4（心的门前石与心光桥）（以上三项挂研究 third_ring，显示「破界环」；ley 另要 04c 地脉核已成型，M §11.1）；心地块 plot@89（source_heart 研究 + 18b 已建，M §6.1 第 23 号；不挂 third_ring）", "owner": "总平面", "since": "0.27", "source": "R §4.11；M §6.1 第 22、23 号、§11.1；V1-08、V1-09", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "ring:bank", "meaning": "两墩电容（third_ring ∧ ring:fit 后提供）", "owner": "终局组", "since": "0.27", "source": "R §4.11", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "crown", "meaning": "桅顶生长：轨道锚 → 源点之冠", "owner": "终局组", "since": "0.27", "source": "R §4.11；B/endgame/README §4", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "core", "meaning": "根位生长：深根 → 地脉核", "owner": "发电组", "since": "0.27", "source": "R §4.11；B/power/tidal_well §7", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "source:base、source:arms", "meaning": "源点之心两段", "owner": "发电组", "since": "0.27", "source": "R §4.11；S §3.8.3(f)", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "ring:12", "meaning": "拱心合拢、乡忆室、控制器、全城锚泊", "owner": "终局组", "since": "0.28", "source": "R §4.11", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "后缀 #1 / #2", "meaning": "地面任务超 2 M EU 的 A 带拆分（勘测完成时由 GroundSplit 确定性地算出）", "owner": "重构组", "since": "0.19", "source": "R §4.11", "status": "新增"},
      {"value": "recast:outer、recast:8、recast:9", "meaning": "非数字的 outer 改 ground:unlay；原地换形一律 reform:<号>", "owner": "—", "since": "不发", "source": "M §10.3.1；R §4.12", "status": "作废"},
      {"value": "site.*、job.swap、plaza.*", "meaning": "永不加（玩家不选场地；NoSiteChoiceTest）", "owner": "—", "since": "不发", "source": "U §2.9.3；R §4.11", "status": "作废"},
      {"value": "grade:top:<k>", "meaning": "削顶：只清不放（检查格 0、步数 0、不成型、不铺地；清场柱 T + 1 → 段的 yTo，不是 grade 柱）。k = 1…n 从 −A 端数起，按 A 累加切段，每段截顶以上的实心格 ≤ 31,250、≤ 2 M EU（单个 A 行就超的再按 R 切）；段边界存任务 Form.Gts（§5.2），勘测完成时写入、之后不变，planFor 重算逐格不变（不是 GroundSplit 那种只靠键后缀的拆法）。重铸：勘测完成后就提供（不等形状门槛），k 依次跑完第 0 批才开放；新园区 campus.hills = grade：重燃后随 anchor 提供，排在全部 ground:* 和 establish:ring 之前。地形走重铸分支（外来机器默认 BLOCKED，矿石按地形清）。grade: 是新前缀，0.16 的计划键里没有", "owner": "总平面", "since": "0.22", "source": "M §10.2.1、§10.3.1；R4-03", "status": "新增"}
    ]},

## 20. 差异表

### 20.1 本账裁定（refactor §4.12 没裁的）

| 编号 | 裁定 | 理由 |
|---|---|---|
| L01 | 跃迁环墩内件以环稿 §7 定稿为准：388 墩台、394 回望窗框**作废**（墩台 = 现有 frame `console` 码 8；回望窗 = 舟壳框 + fitting `glaze` 码 37）；392 只指「钟座」（钟拱 = 舟壳柱 + 拱腹）；**新增 395 墩支管法兰**（`fixture10` · 11，0.20）；389 电容柱芯首发改 0.27（`ring:bank` 才装）。题材件合计 102 行：98 发、1 预留（360）、3 作废（388、394、410） | 同一件东西全城一个码（已有件不另开码）；作废号不复用；395 是块内下一个空位 |
| L02 | 410 白椅**作废**（refactor §4.8 已写「不发」）；白椅 = 舟壳「半 / 阶」的亮面变体；乡忆室与露台的其余 13 件 = `fixture11` 400–409、411–413 | 只增：未发布的号撤回也不复用 |
| L03 | 皮材扩宽批（128–155）按形号升序对应码：舟壳 128–131 = 形 8、9、12、14；玄基 132–137 = 形 7、8、10、13、14、15；`skin_script` 138–145 = 墨 1、3、5、6 / 宣 10、11、14、15；苔铜 146–147 = 形 1、6；汉白玉 148–155 = 形 8–15 | style §3.8.2 只给了段，没给逐码；升序是唯一不需要再查表的规则 |
| L04 | `Buildings` 第 21 行（跃升演出占位）的键 = `ascension` | 与楼稿同名；`leap` 已是研究 id，`Leap` 已是 voyage 键，避开 |
| L05 | 轨道锚单元种类 `cradle` → **`sat_cradle`**（单元名 `sat-1…4`）；`crown_core`、`crown_edge`、`dust` 不变；`crown_edge` 暂按 1 个单元 1 槽登记（sampleSlots 15，见未解决 1） | `fluxecho.unit.cradle` 是 0.16 血契坛宝珠托已发布的种类与 lang 键；同名会让两楼共用一个显示名和 `UnitUse` 分支 |
| L06 | `fluxecho_voyage` 同义字段合并：`Wh` / `WhD` 取代 `Whisper`；`Leap{St, T0, Shot, Part[], Tier}` 取代顶层 `State` / `T0`；`Cele` 取代 `Celebrated`；`Signed:[{U, Name, T}]` 并掉血契坛的 `Pact`；`Pads`（位 0 阳墩、1 阴墩、2 观星台、3 气象站）+ `PadBy[4]` 取代观星台的 `Ign` | 叙事 §3.6 表自己已收录 `Wh`、`Leap{…}`、`Cele`、`Pads`（r2 合并终局键时），被取代的是合并前的残行；三份以上楼稿已按留下的键写逻辑与测试，改一处比改多处少 |
| L07 | 配置一件事一个键：`covenant.signLp` → `voyage.signLp`；`covenant.lpPerMeat` → 已发布的 `bloodmagic.lpPerMeat`；`holo.rehearsalFc` → `voyage.rehearsalFC`；`mobs.euPerHp` → 已发布的 `mobs.euPerHealth`；重构稿的节名 `well.*` / `sails.*` / `heart.*` / `rootnet.*` / `dispatch.*` 跟楼稿：`generators.root.*` / `generators.sails.*` / `generators.heart.*` / `generators.root.net*` / `holo.dispatch*`；补 refactor 漏列的 `client.haze` 等、`archive.*`、`sophon.*`、`holo.*`；电容组（`voyage.capacitorEU` 等）随 `ring:bank` 提到 0.27 | 已发布的不动；`generators` 节是叙事 §9.5 先登记的；楼稿与发电 README 早已按 `generators.*` 写 |
| L08 | lang：楼专属文字一律在 `fluxecho.module.<楼键>.*`（`fluxecho.eco.*`、`fluxecho.fountain.nozzle.*`、`unit.memory_rack.chip` 作废）；窗语 = `fluxecho.codex.whisper.<n>`；计划键任务名 = `fluxecho.build.job.<前缀>`（补 `ring`、`crown`、`core`、`source`） | 叙事 §1.7.1 先登记 `codex.whisper`；`LangPrefixTest` 要求 0.16 之后的键落在登记过的前缀里；`fluxecho.unit.<kind>` 由 `LangKeys.dynamic()` 当种类名检查 |
| L09 | `Ov` 全城保留给「覆层」（中枢 / 档案馆描述包、重铸记录三处已同义）；回响穹顶 tile 的超充键 `Ov` → **`Sov`** | 同一个键全城一个意思；覆层锚点是基类能力，基类以后写 `Ov` 时不能和楼键撞（refactor §4.5「基类以后加键要扫全部楼」） |
| L10 | 回响猎场：`feB13` 保持叙事三级（1 成型、2 放出印记、3 份额备齐），不插「试猎赢」；新增第 32 个记分项 **`feHunt`**（1 第一次试猎赢、2 守门者之证），城志加 `city/hunt_trial`（`feHunt` ≥ 1）、`city/hunt_duel`（`feHunt` ≥ 2） | `leap/hull` 读 `feB13 ≥ 3` = 份额备齐，插级会让它提前满足；只增一个记分项比改阶梯语义安全（待叙事组签，见未解决 2） |
| L11 | 跃迁环五档的形状名（后缀 = 外半径；V1-07 更正「外径」为「外半径」）：`leap_ring`（外半径 32 = 外径 64 档）、`leap_ring_28`、`leap_ring_24`、`leap_ring_20`、`leap_ring_16` | 沿用根位 `well_20` / `well_14` 的「主名 + 尺寸后缀」写法 |
| L12 | `fluxecho:overlay` meta 0–5 = 晶簇、相位板、共鸣窗、记忆丝网、显化外壳、源点辉光 | 叙事 §4.3 覆层表顺序；与研究 `overlay_*` 的解锁顺序一致 |
| L13 | 基类保留键加 `E`：`BookPacket.write` 往 `TileCityModule` 描述包写 `B`、`Bi`、`E` 三个键 | 仓库核对（`library/BookPacket` 第 60–75 行）；refactor §4.5 漏了 `E` |
| L14 | 模组实体注册名 `echo_dragon`（id 0，类 `EntityEchoDragon`）。**R4-02 注**：注册名、id、类名不变，只在用户签猎场未决 11 后由 0.X.1 注册；签字前 §3.4 记「预留」，0.24 不注册（§21 未解决 26） | refactor §4.10 已定；猎场稿写的 `EchoDragon` 是类名口径；猎场 r-p0-1：实体注册名一发布就是永久 ID，用户事后否决也撤不回 |
| L15 | `satellite` meta 0–3（3 = 映日，轨道锚稿只增） | 终局 README §4 写「meta 0–2，无新增」早于锚稿 §3.3 |
| L16 | 新任务 key 的首发版本 = 它的条件第一次可满足的版本（§10.3 是估值）；整条线的 slug 按 refactor §4.11 的版本先建 | BQ 任务能早建，但条件读不到的记分项会让玩家看到做不了的任务 |
| L17 | 材料链物品首发：`phase_core` 0.21；`sail_panel`、`star_plate`、`star_coordinate`、`root_card` 0.25；`satellite`、`source_matter` 0.27；`home_bearing` 0.28 | refactor §4.10「首发 = 第一个用它的楼的版本」逐个落定 |
| L18 | 31 个叙事记分项在 0.17（`ScoreBridge` v1；被砍时整体挪 0.18）一次登记，值随功能才有 | 记分项是字符串常量，一次登记、不随楼逐个加 |
| L19 | 旧任务 key = 83 个（HEAD 76 + 0.16 的 7），一个不删、一个不挪 | 仓库 `quests/build_quests.py` 实数；refactor §4.11 写的「76」少了 0.16 的 7 个 |
| L20 | 不占码的件：冠肋梢座、拱心晶座 = frame `seat`；刻度箍 = 相位合金块；墩台 = frame `console`；回望窗 = 舟壳框 + `glaze`；钟拱 = 舟壳柱 + 拱腹；白椅 = 舟壳亮面变体；栏杆 = `rail`；光梯 = `lift` | style §3.8.3 表后「不占新码的件」+ 环稿 §7 + 锚稿 §3 |
| L21 | `Buildings` 行号 05–19 = 叙事建筑编号（与记分项 `feB05`…`feB19` 同号）：05 穹顶、06 箔仓、07 泉阵、08 秘法庭、09 阙、10 智子、11 沙盘、12 血契坛、13 猎场、14 观星台、15 帆阵、16 气象站、17 水滴、18 桅顶、19 心 | refactor §4.2 只写了 00–04、20、21，而这几行正好就是叙事编号（00 舱 … 04 根位、20 环）；同一套号全城一个，不另排；不用 0.16 `ModuleSpecs` 的运行时登记顺序（它不进存档、也没有舱、中枢、环） |
| L22 | 重铸前勘测与削顶的名（总平面 r-p0-1 只给了建议，削顶段的键连名都没给）：① 削顶段边界 = 任务 **`Form.Gts`**（int[5]），放 `BuildJob.Form`、不放 `BuildJob` 本体；② 外来机器的二次确认 = 动作 **`build.allow_foreign`**（0.19，随 `Rx`）；③ [重新勘测]、[我已备份]、[放弃重建 · 取回] = **`recast.survey`**、**`recast.backed_up`**、**`recast.abandon`**（0.22）；④ 园区键照总平面建议名 `Rx` | ① 段边界决定计划，`Form` 的既有约定是「客户端照它算出和服务端一样的计划」（H `BuildJob.Form` 类注释），投影要它；平铺进 `writeSync` 的撞键风险由 `TaskKeysTest` 守。② 总平面「版本」段把 [拆进回收箱] 和 `Rx` 同放 F15（0.19），新园区 `Mp` = 1 的地面任务从第一项起就会遇到外来机器；动作只登「预留」，0.19 的 `Rx` 就没有写入口。名照 P03 现有的 `build.*`。③ 名照总平面与批评稿的建议，同属 C03；动作键和配置键是两个名空间，`recast.*` 不撞配置 `recast.storeKb`（同 `observatory.pullFromVault`） |

25. 中枢描述包要不要带 `Capsule`（B/nexus/drift_capsule 未决 15）：`ABSORB` 之后到第一次成型之间晶核由中枢画，楼稿倾向不加键、由已同步的任务状态（带 `Form.Ab` 的 `establish` 已执行 `ABSORB`、未成型）推出；要加就在 0.19 接口提交前登 §5.3。中枢组 / 界面组 / 引擎组定。（V2 新增）
26. `echo_dragon` 只在用户签猎场未决 11（BRIEF §5「不做敌人」的例外）后注册；`hunt.trials` / `hunt.duels` 默认 false，签字后由 0.X.1 经 `Config.upgrade()` 只把仍是旧默认 false 的翻成 true，同时 §3.4 改「新增」并注册实体、§10.3 两条城志改常显；不签就永远停在「预留」（B/blood-hunt/hunting_ground 未决 11、§8 #26）。（R4-02 新增）

## 22. 规则

### 22.1 只增

1. **已发布的行一个字都不改**（值、含义、归属都不改）。0.16.0 标签里的全部 ID 已经在本账；以后每打一个标签，那一版的「新增」行同样冻结。
2. **作废的值永不复用**：号段里留洞也不补。已发布后退役的值（GT offset 3、12 这类）记「已发布 · 退役」，同样不复用。
3. **预留只能变成新增**（写明用途和首发版本），不能挪作他用；「新增」在发布前可以改（改了回写本账并在差异表留一行），发布后冻结。
4. **号段内按顺序取下一个空位，不跳号**；码段之间的余量（各块余位、238–255、416–1023、`module_core2` 82–95、S→C 17 起）是有意留的，不许填别的组的东西。
5. 版本还没到的 ID：消息号只有常量、没有处理器；题材件 meta 不注册物品、不进创造栏；`Buildings` 行是 `LegacyKit`；配置节可以有空类，但键不写进 cfg。

### 22.2 怎么登记一个新 ID

1. 先查本账：同一件东西已经有码 / 键 / 名，就用它（「同一件东西全城一个码」）；现有件能做的不开新码（L20）。
2. 撞了按顺序裁：**已发布的不动 → 先登记的优先 → 同一件东西全城一个码 → 相邻码段留余量**。
3. 在本账对应的表末尾追加一行：值、含义、归属、首发版本、出处、状态（「预留」或「新增」）；楼稿只引用账上的值，不再自报号段。
4. 版本接口提交（refactor §⑥ D0.5）里，整合者把本版要用的行定成「新增 · 本版」，同步 `batches.csv`；打标签的那个提交里改成「已发布」。
5. 机器可读：§19 的 JSON 是本账的同构导出，入库后由它生成 `ids.csv`（一行一值：类别 · 名 · 值 · 首发 · 归谁 · 状态）。

### 22.3 谁改账

| 时期 | 谁 | 怎么改 |
|---|---|---|
| 设计期（现在） | 记账者 | 只改本稿；楼稿、组 README 按差异表各自改 |
| 0.17.0 起 | **整合者**（`docs/dev/ids.md`、`ids.csv`、`batches.csv` 都在红区） | 楼代理、组代理要新 ID 提 RFC；整合者追加行、在接口提交里定版、在标签提交里改「已发布」 |
| 任何时候 | 没有人 | 改已发布行、复用作废值、跳号、在别的组的块里取号 |

### 22.4 测试怎么守这本账

| 测试 | 守什么 |
|---|---|
| `IdFreezeTest` | `ids.csv` 与源码常量逐行相等；已发布行不变；作废值没被注册；预留的注册名（`echo_dragon`、`rain_veil`）没被注册（R4-02） |
| `NetKindsTest` | 消息号常量与 §4 逐行相等、两两不重复、旧号不变 |
| `PartsTest`（style §3.8.3 (e) 六条，第 6 条改 ≥ 0.20.0） | 码段不重叠；(方块, meta) ↔ 码一一对应；旧码不变；留空绑定 meta 没注册物品 |
| `BatchPlanTest` | 每个 ID 的首发版本与 `batches.csv` 一致 |
| `NodeSinceTest` | 83 颗星的 `since` 与 §9 / refactor §2.3 一致 |
| `DescKeysTest` | 各楼 `BuildingKit.nbtKeys()` 不和 §5.1 基类键重名 |
| `TaskKeysTest`（R4-09；由 R3-01 的 `FormKeysTest` 扩名，各稿写 `FormKeysTest` 的都指它；随 0.19 `Form.Ab` 的接口提交进，以后任务层每加一个键都要过） | 0.17 起新增的任务层键——`BuildJob` 本体的 `Fl2`（0.20），`BuildJob.Form` 的 `Ab`（0.19）、`Fsn`（0.21）、`Gts`（0.22），以及以后新增的任务键和 `Campus.writeSync` 同步键——两两不同，且 ∩（0.16 `BuildJob.write` 的键 ∪ 0.16 `Campus.writeSync` 的键）= ∅。0.16 两组键集从 0.16.0 源码抽出、冻结在 `ids.csv`（`BuildJob` 52 个，含 `Form.write` 写的 `Pf` `Pd` `Sd` `Rs`；`writeSync` 33 个，含 `Sn`）；新键集取 §5.2 标「任务」的新增行的物理键。`Form` 的新键同时平铺进任务 NBT 和 `writeSync`，所以两组都要比；0.16 的 `Form` 键本来就在 `BuildJob` 那一组里，新键撞它们同样算撞。同一个类另查园区层：中枢 `Campus` 子表的新键（`Skn` `Mp` `Rk` `Rx` `Dispatch` `HoloAwake`）两两不同、不撞 0.16 `Campus` 子表的键，`Ledger` 子复合的新键（`LegAcct` `Parts2`）不撞 `Raw` `Parts`（`Rx` 随 0.19 进）。`DescKeysTest` 只管楼的 `nbtKeys()` 对基类键，管不到任务 / 同步 / 园区这一层 |
| `LangShardTest`、`LangPrefixTest` | 一个键只在一个分片；0.16 之后的新键落在 §8 的前缀里 |
| `UnitFreezeTest`、`UnitLayoutTest` | 0.16 单元表前缀不变，只在末尾追加（§16） |
| `ConfigTest` | 旧键全在、默认值不变（迁移过的除外） |
| `ModuleStatusOrdinalTest`、`PlanKeyTest`、`NoSiteChoiceTest`、`QuestPackTest`、`FacadeFreezeTest` | §14、§13（含 `grade:top:<k>` 的分派与非法参数）、§17 的禁用键、§10、门面（§3.5 的签名指纹；0.20 冻结的 `ShipTide.sig` 含 `windowTide` `nextAbove` `factor`，R4-01） |

### r-final（终验第 1 轮，2026-10-16）

依据：`critique/final-check-1.md`（终验者逐条核对第 4 轮 14 条 P0 在被点名的每一份文档里的落地情况）。本账四条 P0（r4-feasibility #3、r4-gameplay #2、r4-rules #2、#6）在 r-p0-2 已全部落地，终验复核：§3.4 `echo_dragon` 预留、§3.5 `ShipTide` 三方法、§5.2 `Rx` / `Form.Gts`、§7 `campus.hills` 取值表、§8 `.grade`、§13.1 `grade:top:<k>`、§17.2 四个动作键、§10.3 两条城志「完成后才显示」、§21 #26、§22.4 `TaskKeysTest` / `IdFreezeTest`——正文与 §19 JSON 逐节计数一致（终验脚本重新解析 JSON：各节行数与状态分布和 §0 表逐格相同，合计 1076 / 277 / 730 / 40 / 29）。本轮只改了两类东西，**没有新增、删改任何 ID 行，JSON 和 §0 不动**：

| # | 位置 | 原文（摘） | 现在 | 为什么 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | §21 未解决 16 的 `ShipTide.moonOf(night)` 一项；差异 #244 | 「`moonOf` 仍挂在这里」「`moonOf` 仍走 RFC」 | 划掉并注「r-final 关闭：refactor r-p0-2 在 §4.3 表后『纯逻辑、不是门面』段定为 `logic/ledger/ShipTide` 的静态纯函数、不进门面」 | refactor 已裁、发电 README 本来就给过这条路；账继续挂 RFC 等于和 refactor 两说（终验按「以 refactor 与账为准，账自身有错就改账」处理）。§3.5 不加方法，`ShipTide.sig` 不变 |
| 2 | 差异表 #229–#236、#242–#246 | 指向楼稿 / 总平面 / 界面稿的同步行，r-p0-2 时还没人执行 | 各行末尾注「〔r-final：已执行〕」：总平面 `Form.Gts`、`Rx`、动作键与 `Fsn`（#229、#234–#236）；潮汐井、谐振塔、穹顶、生态穹的 `Fsn`（#230–#233）；界面 §2.9.3 四个动作（#242）；跃升未决 27（#243）；发电 README（#244）；潮汐井 ⑥（#245）；观星台两处引账（#246） | 各稿 r-final 已照改（终验逐处 grep 过） |
